def compute_smallest_primes_for_cs(cs, multM_multiplier=None, limit=200000):
    results = {}
    primes = list(sp.primerange(3, limit))
    pow3_mod = {p: pow(3, K, p) for p in primes}
    for c in cs:
        for p in primes:
            if (pow3_mod[p] + c - 1) % p == 0:
                results[c] = p
                break
        else:
            results[c] = None
    if multM_multiplier is not None:
        mult = multM_multiplier + 1
        for p in primes:
            if (mult * pow3_mod[p] - 1) % p == 0:
                results['mult'] = p
                break
        else:
            results['mult'] = None
    return results

[CODE] (turn 1):
import math, itertools, sympy as sp

def f_bruteforce(n):
    best = None
    best_trip = None
    # enumerate a<b<c distinct positive integers sum n
    # limit a up to n-3
    for a in range(1, n-1):
        for b in range(a+1, n):
            c = n - a - b
            if c <= b: 
                continue
            # distinct automatically because c > b > a
            l = sp.ilcm(a,b,c)
            if best is None or l < best:
                best = l
                best_trip = (a,b,c)
    return best, best_trip

# compute for n up to 30
vals = {}
for n in range(6, 31):
    best,_ = f_bruteforce(n)
    vals[n]=best
vals[:10]

[CODE] (turn 5):
def min_ratio(limit):
    best = None
    best_trip = None
    for a in range(1, limit+1):
        for b in range(a+1, limit+1):
            for c in range(b+1, limit+1):
                s = a+b+c
                l = sp.ilcm(a,b,c)
                r = sp.Rational(l, s)
                if best is None or r < best:
                    best = r
                    best_trip = (a,b,c)
    return best, best_trip

best, trip = min_ratio(30)
best, trip

[CODE] (turn 10):
def min_ratio_for_sum(s):
    best = None
    best_trip = None
    for a in range(1, s-1):
        for b in range(a+1, s):
            c = s - a - b
            if c <= b:
                continue
            l = sp.ilcm(a,b,c)
            r = sp.Rational(l, s)
            if best is None or r < best:
                best = r
                best_trip = (a,b,c)
    return best, best_trip

def compute_min_ratios(limit):
    res = {}
    for s in range(6, limit+1):
        r,trip = min_ratio_for_sum(s)
        res[s] = (r,trip)
    return res

ratios = compute_min_ratios(30)
for s in sorted(ratios):
    print(s, ratios[s][0], ratios[s][1])

def min_ratio_for_sum_bruteforce(s):
    best = None
    for a in range(1, s-1):
        for b in range(a+1, s):
            c = s - a - b
            if c <= b:
                continue
            l = sp.ilcm(a,b,c)
            r = sp.Rational(l, s)
            if best is None or r < best:
                best = r
    return best

def min_ratio_for_sum_bruteforce(s):
    best = None
    for a in range(1, s-1):
        for b in range(a+1, s):
            c = s - a - b
            if c <= b:
                continue
            l = sp.ilcm(a,b,c)
            r = sp.Rational(l, s)
            if best is None or r < best:
                best = r
    return best

def minimal_ratio_S(c, E, max_s=200):
    # c can be integer (including possibly expression like 4M)
    # We'll handle c as either integer or tuple indicating multiple of M
    # For simplicity, we treat c as integer (since 4M is huge, we compute using M_mod_s)
    # We'll pass a flag for c_type: 0 for integer, 1 for multiple of M (i.e., c = k*M)

[CODE] (turn 19):
def minimal_ratio_for_given_c(c_int, kM, E, max_s=200):
    # c_int is integer part not multiple of M (for cases where c is integer)
    # kM is integer multiplier for M (0 if none, else e.g., 4 for 4M)
    # S = M + kM*M + c_int = (1+kM)*M + c_int
    # compute minimal ratio among s dividing S
    best_ratio = None
    best_s = None
    for s in range(6, max_s+1):
        # compute M mod s
        M_mod = pow(3, E, s)
        S_mod = ((1 + kM) * M_mod + c_int) % s
        if S_mod == 0:
            r = r_s[s]
            if best_ratio is None or r < best_ratio:
                best_ratio = r
                best_s = s
    return best_ratio, best_s

# compute E
E = math.factorial(2025)
# test for each c

    (0,0), # c_int=0, kM=0
    (0,4), # c_int=0, kM=4 (c=4M)
    (1848374,0),
    (10162574,0),
    (265710644,0),
    (44636594,0)
]
for c_int,kM in c_list:
    ratio, s = minimal_ratio_for_given_c(c_int,kM,E, max_s=200)
    print(c_int,kM, ratio, s)

[CODE] (turn 23):
def compute_min_ratio_up_to(N):
    min_ratio = None
    min_s = None
    for s in range(6, N+1):
        # skip if s divisible by 6,7,10,11? Actually we want all s.
        r = r_s.get(s)
        if r is None:
            r = min_ratio_for_sum_bruteforce(s)
            r_s[s] = r
        if min_ratio is None or r < min_ratio:
            min_ratio = r
            min_s = s
    return min_ratio, min_s

# compute up to 500
min_ratio_500, min_s_500 = compute_min_ratio_up_to(500)
min_ratio_500, min_s_500

        # compute M mod s
        M_mod = pow(3, E, s)
        S_mod = ((1 + kM) * M_mod + c_int) % s
        if S_mod == 0:
            r = r_s[s]  # precomputed
            if best_ratio is None or r < best_ratio:
                best_ratio = r
                best_s = s
    return best_ratio, best_s

[CODE] (turn 26):
def find_s_with_ratio_below(threshold, max_s=2000):
    result = []
    for s in range(6, max_s+1):
        r = r_s.get(s)
        if r is None:
            r = min_ratio_for_sum_bruteforce(s)
            r_s[s] = r
        if r < threshold:
            result.append((s, r))
    return result

threshold = Fraction(30,47)  # approx 0.6383
cand = find_s_with_ratio_below(threshold, max_s=2000)
len(cand)
